Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 42, Step 5, question 2: "Were you self-employed at any time in 2025, or are you filing Schedule SE because you were a member of the clergy or you had church employee income, or are you filing Schedule C as a statutory employee? Yes. Skip question 3 and Step 6; go to Worksheet B." Triage executable-triage-v2 (triage/eic.deepseek.json; 1,111 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 128 partial records; 1,739 comparisons): 696 of 1,739 agree; 820 candidate-unknown (292 on completed returns), 150 value (42 on completed returns), 73 candidate-refuses. The candidate counts only filers of Schedule SE (person.files_se), Schedules C with tips or overtime for Schedule 1-A (s1.sc_1a_count) and any person.se_screen answer as "clergy". A Schedule C filer with net earnings under $400 (or a loss) was self-employed but files no Schedule SE; person.se_screen also covers partnership, farm and other self-employment, not the clergy; and the interview does not ask person.se_screen of filers without a Schedule C, so the candidate needs an answer the source does not. Witnesses: classm.f2441.1.deepseek (completed; wages only) project no, candidate unknown (person[taxpayer].se_screen); classm.f5695.2 (completed) project no, candidate yes (se_screen read as clergy); classm.asset.4 project yes (a Schedule C), candidate no. The candidate-refuses are Schedule C gates on refused returns (scope).

Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 40, Step 1, question 1: "If, in 2025: 3 or more children who have valid SSNs lived with you, is the amount on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b, less than $61,555 ($68,675 if married filing jointly)? ... No. STOP You can't take the credit." i1040gi p. 46, Worksheet A: "Use this worksheet only if you answered “No” to Step 5, question 2. Otherwise, use Worksheet B." Triage executable-triage-v2 (triage/eic.deepseek.json; 1,111 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 128 partial records; 1,739 comparisons): 558 of 1,739 agree; 1,115 candidate-unknown (510 on completed returns), 43 candidate-refuses (8 on completed returns), 15 value (10 on completed returns), 6 candidate-shows-skipped, 2 project-refuses. The candidate figures line 6 on every return (stage16.cleanup.mut.clwb_three_figured, completed, hidden; candidate $461.00) and refuses completed returns on its eic.clergy gate (classm.f5695.2). Worksheet B value differences (stage14.eic.wsb_loss $614.00 / $649.00; classm.sli.4 $0.00 / $503.00) are the merged-line interface gap with the candidate's wrong routing, as on line 2. Project refusals cited (i1040gi p. 44): scope.

Reported, not judged: the project often refuses before a worksheet is used, on the line that chooses it, which a per-line comparison cannot see.

| eic.check_27c | eic.clergy, eic.h_investment_4797, household.credit_disallowed, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | eic.clergy, eic.h_investment_4797, household.credit_disallowed |

| 2 | eic.clergy, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income | eic.clergy |

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
lines:
  - id: eic.step5_line1
    printed: "(Step 5, 1)"
    label: "Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 1z."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: "f1040.line1z"}
    refuses: [eic.clergy]
  - id: eic.step5_line2
    printed: "(Step 5, 2)"
    label: "Enter the Medicaid waiver payment amounts excluded from income on Schedule 1, line 8s, unless you choose to include these amounts in earned income, in which case enter -0-. See the instructions for Schedule 1, line 8s."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: "ss.zero"}
    refuses: [scope.other_income]
    note: "No separate Schedule 1 line 8s input is provided; the line is zero unless scope.other_income refuses."
  - id: eic.step5_line3
    printed: "(Step 5, 3)"
    label: "Subtract line 2 from line 1."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: "eic.step5_line1", take: "eic.step5_line2"}
  - id: eic.step5_line4
    printed: "(Step 5, 4)"
    label: "Enter all of your nontaxable combat pay if you elect to include it in earned income. Also enter the amount of your nontaxable combat pay on line 1i of Form 1040 or 1040-SR. See Combat pay, nontaxable, later."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: "ss.zero"}
    refuses: [s8812.combat_pay]
    note: "The amount of nontaxable combat pay is not separately provided; the line refuses when s8812.combat_pay is true and is otherwise zero."
  - id: eic.earned_income
    printed: "(Step 5, 5)"
    label: "Add lines 3 and 4. This is your earned income."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: ["eic.step5_line3", "eic.step5_line4"]}
  - id: eic.wsb_4b
    printed: "4b"
    label: "Combine lines 1e, 2c, 3, and 4a. This is your total earned income."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: ["eic.earned_income", "eic.h_se_total"]}
    note: "Uses person.eic_se as the aggregate of Worksheet B lines 1e, 2c, and 3; the catalog does not expose those components separately."
  - id: eic.wsb_6
    printed: "6 (eic.wsb_6)"
    label: "Enter your total earned income from Part 4, line 4b."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: "eic.wsb_4b", take: {const: 0}}
    note: "Worksheet B line 4b zero or less is a STOP; the nonnegative interface is floored at zero."
  - id: eic.line1
    printed: "1"
    label: "Enter your earned income from Step 5."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: "eic.earned_income"}
  - id: eic.line2
    printed: "2"
    label: "Look up the amount on line 1 above in the EIC Table to find the credit. Be sure you use the correct column for your filing status and the number of qualifying children you have who have valid SSNs. Enter the credit here."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: ["eic.qualifying_children", {const: 0, type: Count}]}
          then: "eic.h_lookup0"
        - guard: {op: test, eq: ["eic.qualifying_children", {const: 1, type: Count}]}
          then: "eic.h_lookup1"
        - guard: {op: test, eq: ["eic.qualifying_children", {const: 2, type: Count}]}
          then: "eic.h_lookup2"
        - guard: {op: test, ge: ["eic.qualifying_children", {const: 3, type: Count}]}
          then: "eic.h_lookup3"
  - id: eic.line3
    printed: "3"
    label: "Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: "f1040.line11b"}
  - id: eic.line4
    printed: "4"
    label: "Are the amounts on lines 3 and 1 the same?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: ["eic.line3", "eic.line1"]}
  - id: eic.line5
    printed: "5"
    label: "If you have: No qualifying children who have valid SSNs, is the amount on line 3 less than $10,620 ($17,730 if married filing jointly)? 1 or more qualifying children who have valid SSNs, is the amount on line 3 less than $23,350 ($30,470 if married filing jointly)? Yes. Leave line 5 blank; enter the amount from line 2 on line 6. No. Look up the amount on line 3 in the EIC Table to find the credit. Be sure you use the correct column for your filing status and the number of qualifying children you have who have valid SSNs. Enter the credit here. Look at the amounts on lines 5 and 2. Then, enter the smaller amount on line 6."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_line3_below_threshold", {const: true}]}
          then: "eic.h_zero"
        - guard: {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_line3_below_threshold", {const: false}]}
          then: "eic.h_line3_credit"
    note: "Line 5 is left blank when line 3 is below the threshold; modeled as zero, with line 6 then using line 2 per the instructions."
  - id: eic.line6
    printed: "6 (eic.line6)"
    label: "This is your earned income credit."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: ["eic.line4", {const: true}]}
          then: "eic.line2"
        - guard: {op: test, all: [
            {op: test, eq: ["eic.line4", {const: false}]},
            {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_line3_below_threshold", {const: true}]}
          ]}
          then: "eic.line2"
        - guard: {op: test, all: [
            {op: test, eq: ["eic.line4", {const: false}]},
            {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_line3_below_threshold", {const: false}]}
          ]}
          then: "eic.h_line6_smaller"
  - id: eic.h_se_total
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: "person", field: "person.eic_se"}
  - id: eic.h_lookup0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: "eic_0", key: "eic.line1", status: "f1040.status"}
  - id: eic.h_lookup1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: "eic_1", key: "eic.line1", status: "f1040.status"}
  - id: eic.h_lookup2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: "eic_2", key: "eic.line1", status: "f1040.status"}
  - id: eic.h_lookup3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: "eic_3", key: "eic.line1", status: "f1040.status"}
  - id: eic.h_line3_lookup0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: "eic_0", key: "eic.line3", status: "f1040.status"}
  - id: eic.h_line3_lookup1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: "eic_1", key: "eic.line3", status: "f1040.status"}
  - id: eic.h_line3_lookup2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: "eic_2", key: "eic.line3", status: "f1040.status"}
  - id: eic.h_line3_lookup3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: "eic_3", key: "eic.line3", status: "f1040.status"}
  - id: eic.h_line3_credit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: ["eic.qualifying_children", {const: 0, type: Count}]}
          then: "eic.h_line3_lookup0"
        - guard: {op: test, eq: ["eic.qualifying_children", {const: 1, type: Count}]}
          then: "eic.h_line3_lookup1"
        - guard: {op: test, eq: ["eic.qualifying_children", {const: 2, type: Count}]}
          then: "eic.h_line3_lookup2"
        - guard: {op: test, ge: ["eic.qualifying_children", {const: 3, type: Count}]}
          then: "eic.h_line3_lookup3"
  - id: eic.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: "ss.zero"}
  - id: eic.h_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: ["f1040.status", {const: "mfj"}]}
  - id: eic.h_thr0_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: "eic.ws_threshold0.other"}
  - id: eic.h_thr0_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: "eic.ws_threshold0.joint"}
  - id: eic.h_thr1_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: "eic.ws_threshold1.other"}
  - id: eic.h_thr1_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: "eic.ws_threshold1.joint"}
  - id: eic.h_threshold0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_joint", {const: true}]}
          then: "eic.h_thr0_joint"
        - guard: {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_joint", {const: false}]}
          then: "eic.h_thr0_other"
  - id: eic.h_threshold1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_joint", {const: true}]}
          then: "eic.h_thr1_joint"
        - guard: {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_joint", {const: false}]}
          then: "eic.h_thr1_other"
  - id: eic.h_threshold
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: ["eic.qualifying_children", {const: 0, type: Count}]}
          then: "eic.h_threshold0"
        - guard: {op: test, ge: ["eic.qualifying_children", {const: 1, type: Count}]}
          then: "eic.h_threshold1"
  - id: eic.h_line3_below_threshold
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, lt: ["eic.line3", "eic.h_threshold"]}
  - id: eic.h_line6_smaller
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: ["eic.line2", "eic.line5"]}
  - id: eic.qualifying_children
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: "dependent"
      field: "dependent.one"
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: ["dependent.eic_child", {const: true}]}
          - {op: test, eq: ["dependent.eic_ssn_child", {const: true}]}
  - id: eic.h_qc_us_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: "dependent"
      field: "dependent.one"
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: ["dependent.eic_child", {const: true}]}
          - {op: test, eq: ["dependent.us_half", {const: true}]}
  - id: eic.h_se_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: "person"
      field: "person.one"
      where: {op: test, eq: ["person.files_se", {const: true}]}
  - id: eic.h_se_screen_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: "person"
      field: "person.one"
      where: {op: test, eq: ["person.se_screen", {const: true}]}
  - id: eic.clergy
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Are you filing Schedule SE because you were a member of the clergy or you had church employee income of $108.28 or more?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: ["eic.h_se_screen_count", {const: 0, type: Count}]}
    note: "No separate clergy/church-employee line is provided; uses person.se_screen as the available screening proxy."
  - id: eic.self_employed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Were you self-employed at any time in 2025, or are you filing Schedule SE because you were a member of the clergy or you had church employee income, or are you filing Schedule C as a statutory employee?"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, gt: ["eic.h_se_count", {const: 0, type: Count}]}
        - {op: test, gt: ["s1.sc_1a_count", {const: 0, type: Count}]}
        - {op: test, eq: ["eic.clergy", {const: true}]}
  - id: eic.h_age_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: "person"
      field: "person.one"
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: ["person.born_after_1960", {const: true}]}
          - {op: test, eq: ["person.born_before_2001", {const: true}]}
  - id: eic.h_age_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: ["eic.h_age_count", {const: 0, type: Count}]}
  - id: eic.h_married_not_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: ["household.married", {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: ["f1040.status", {const: "mfs"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: ["f1040.status", {const: "hoh"}]}
  - id: eic.h_step3_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      not:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_married_not_joint", {const: true}]}
          - {op: test, eq: ["eic.same_home", {const: true}]}
          - {op: test, eq: ["eic.legally_separated", {const: false}]}
  - id: eic.h_children_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: ["eic.qualifying_children", {const: 0, type: Count}]}
  - id: eic.h_children_positive
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: ["eic.qualifying_children", {const: 0, type: Count}]}
  - id: eic.h_no_child_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_married_not_joint", {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_age_ok", {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: ["eic.us_home", {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: ["eic.qc_of_another", {const: false}]}
  - id: eic.h_with_child_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: ["eic.qc_of_another", {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_step3_ok", {const: true}]}
  - id: eic.h_children_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_children_zero", {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_no_child_ok", {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_children_positive", {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_with_child_ok", {const: true}]}
  - id: eic.h_line7a_nonneg
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: "f1040.line7a", take: {const: 0}}
  - id: eic.h_investment_income
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: ["f1040.line2a", "f1040.line2b", "f1040.line3b", "eic.h_line7a_nonneg"]}
  - id: eic.h_investment_over
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: ["eic.h_investment_income", {param: "eic.investment_limit"}]}
  - id: eic.h_investment_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, le: ["eic.h_investment_income", {param: "eic.investment_limit"}]}
  - id: eic.h_investment_4797
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_investment_over", {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: ["sd.other_forms", {const: true}]}
  - id: eic.h_limit0_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: "eic.limit0.other"}
  - id: eic.h_limit0_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: "eic.limit0.joint"}
  - id: eic.h_limit1_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: "eic.limit1.other"}
  - id: eic.h_limit1_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: "eic.limit1.joint"}
  - id: eic.h_limit2_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: "eic.limit2.other"}
  - id: eic.h_limit2_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: "eic.limit2.joint"}
  - id: eic.h_limit3_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: "eic.limit3.other"}
  - id: eic.h_limit3_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: "eic.limit3.joint"}
  - id: eic.h_limit0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_joint", {const: true}]}
          then: "eic.h_limit0_joint"
        - guard: {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_joint", {const: false}]}
          then: "eic.h_limit0_other"
  - id: eic.h_limit1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_joint", {const: true}]}
          then: "eic.h_limit1_joint"
        - guard: {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_joint", {const: false}]}
          then: "eic.h_limit1_other"
  - id: eic.h_limit2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_joint", {const: true}]}
          then: "eic.h_limit2_joint"
        - guard: {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_joint", {const: false}]}
          then: "eic.h_limit2_other"
  - id: eic.h_limit3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_joint", {const: true}]}
          then: "eic.h_limit3_joint"
        - guard: {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_joint", {const: false}]}
          then: "eic.h_limit3_other"
  - id: eic.h_income_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: ["eic.qualifying_children", {const: 0, type: Count}]}
          then: "eic.h_limit0"
        - guard: {op: test, eq: ["eic.qualifying_children", {const: 1, type: Count}]}
          then: "eic.h_limit1"
        - guard: {op: test, eq: ["eic.qualifying_children", {const: 2, type: Count}]}
          then: "eic.h_limit2"
        - guard: {op: test, ge: ["eic.qualifying_children", {const: 3, type: Count}]}
          then: "eic.h_limit3"
  - id: eic.h_line3_below_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, lt: ["eic.line3", "eic.h_income_limit"]}
  - id: eic.h_earned_for_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: ["eic.self_employed", {const: true}]}
          then: "eic.wsb_4b"
        - guard: {op: test, eq: ["eic.self_employed", {const: false}]}
          then: "eic.earned_income"
  - id: eic.h_earned_below_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, lt: ["eic.h_earned_for_limit", "eic.h_income_limit"]}
  - id: eic.h_se_positive
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: ["eic.wsb_4b", {const: 0}]}
  - id: eic.h_self_employed_positive
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: ["eic.self_employed", {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_se_positive", {const: true}]}
  - id: eic.h_eligible
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: ["household.all_eic_ssn", {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_line3_below_limit", {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_investment_ok", {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_children_ok", {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_earned_below_limit", {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_self_employed_positive", {const: true}]}
  - id: eic.h_wsb7_lookup0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: "eic_0", key: "eic.wsb_6", status: "f1040.status"}
  - id: eic.h_wsb7_lookup1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: "eic_1", key: "eic.wsb_6", status: "f1040.status"}
  - id: eic.h_wsb7_lookup2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: "eic_2", key: "eic.wsb_6", status: "f1040.status"}
  - id: eic.h_wsb7_lookup3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: "eic_3", key: "eic.wsb_6", status: "f1040.status"}
  - id: eic.h_wsb7_credit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: ["eic.qualifying_children", {const: 0, type: Count}]}
          then: "eic.h_wsb7_lookup0"
        - guard: {op: test, eq: ["eic.qualifying_children", {const: 1, type: Count}]}
          then: "eic.h_wsb7_lookup1"
        - guard: {op: test, eq: ["eic.qualifying_children", {const: 2, type: Count}]}
          then: "eic.h_wsb7_lookup2"
        - guard: {op: test, ge: ["eic.qualifying_children", {const: 3, type: Count}]}
          then: "eic.h_wsb7_lookup3"
  - id: eic.h_wsb8
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: "eic.line3"}
  - id: eic.h_wsb9
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_wsb8", "eic.wsb_6"]}
  - id: eic.h_wsb10_below_threshold
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, lt: ["eic.h_wsb8", "eic.h_threshold"]}
  - id: eic.h_wsb10
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_wsb10_below_threshold", {const: true}]}
          then: "eic.h_zero"
        - guard: {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_wsb10_below_threshold", {const: false}]}
          then: "eic.h_line3_credit"
  - id: eic.h_wsb11_smaller
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: ["eic.h_wsb7_credit", "eic.h_wsb10"]}
  - id: eic.h_wsb11
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_wsb9", {const: true}]}
          then: "eic.h_wsb7_credit"
        - guard: {op: test, all: [
            {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_wsb9", {const: false}]},
            {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_wsb10_below_threshold", {const: true}]}
          ]}
          then: "eic.h_wsb7_credit"
        - guard: {op: test, all: [
            {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_wsb9", {const: false}]},
            {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_wsb10_below_threshold", {const: false}]}
          ]}
          then: "eic.h_wsb11_smaller"
  - id: eic.h_credit_selected
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: ["eic.self_employed", {const: true}]}
          then: "eic.h_wsb11"
        - guard: {op: test, eq: ["eic.self_employed", {const: false}]}
          then: "eic.line6"
  - id: eic.credit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Earned income credit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_eligible", {const: true}]}
          then: "eic.h_credit_selected"
        - guard: {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_eligible", {const: false}]}
          then: "eic.h_zero"
    refuses: [scope.other_income, scope.schedule1, eic.h_investment_4797, household.credit_disallowed]
  - id: eic.check_27c
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Check the box on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 27c"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: ["eic.credit", {const: 0}]}
    note: "True when the computed EIC is zero, matching the STOP instructions that direct the filer to check line 27c."
  - id: eic.separated_box
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Check the box in the Dependents section that discusses the special rule for separated spouses"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: ["eic.h_married_not_joint", {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, gt: ["eic.h_qc_us_count", {const: 0, type: Count}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: ["eic.same_home", {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: ["eic.legally_separated", {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: ["eic.qc_of_another", {const: false}]}
    note: "Derived from the Step 3 separated-spouse questions and the qualifying-child US residence test."
```
